Rounding out the trials yesterday were two “eternal” formats—Modern and Legacy. For those of you clamoring for the latest iterations of your favorite archetypes, *or* a 61-card Kiln Fiend list, look no further than this here article!

***Modern***

#### David Li's Death's Shadow Aggro – GP Richmond Trial Winner
